# Phase 2 Action 2.6 - Agent, Tool, Action, MCP, A2A, and Connector Evidence

## Observation class

**SOURCE-OBSERVED STATIC CANDIDATES ONLY. RUNTIME EXECUTION, TOOL AUTHORIZATION, MCP CONNECTIVITY, A2A COMMUNICATION, AND CONNECTOR TRUST ARE UNVERIFIED.**

## Scope

This action inventories tracked source candidates associated with agents, tools, executable actions, MCP clients, MCP servers, A2A components, and connectors/integrations.

No agent was executed, no tool was invoked, no MCP connection was established, and no external connector was contacted.

## Method

- Enumerated tracked files using `git ls-files`.
- Examined text files up to 2 MiB.
- Skipped binary files.
- Excluded `docs/security/` from candidate discovery.
- Recorded candidate paths and match counts rather than credentials, payloads, or external connection details.
- Keyword matches are discovery evidence only and do not prove runtime availability, privilege, authorization, or isolation.

- Tracked text files scanned: **7451**
- Large files skipped: **1**

## Security interpretation

This inventory identifies source locations for later agentic security analysis. It does not establish which agents, tools, actions, MCP components, A2A components, or connectors are active or reachable at runtime.

Later controlled testing must verify tool authorization, least privilege, argument validation, user intent binding, agent identity, MCP trust boundaries, connector permissions, cross-tenant isolation, code execution controls, and failure paths.
